3</a></i>.</div></div></div>\n<p>In 1966, Iron Man was featured in a series of cartoons.<sup id="cite_ref-140" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-140">&#91;140&#93;</a></sup> In 1981, Iron Man guest appeared in <i><a href="/wiki/Spider-Man_and_His_Amazing_Friends" title="Spider-Man and His Amazing Friends">Spider-Man and His Amazing Friends</a></i>, but only as Tony Stark.<sup id="cite_ref-141" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-141">&#91;141&#93;</a></sup> He went on to feature again in his own series in the 1990s as part of the <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel_Action_Hour" class="mw-redirect" title="Marvel Action Hour">Marvel Action Hour</a></i> with the Fantastic Four; <a href="/wiki/Robert_Hays" title="Robert Hays">Robert Hays</a> provided his voice in these animated cartoons. Iron Man makes an appearance in the episode "Shell Games" of <i><a href="/wiki/Fantastic_Four:_World%27s_Greatest_Heroes" title="Fantastic Four: World&#39;s Greatest Heroes">Fantastic Four: World\'s Greatest Heroes</a>.</i>\nApart from comic books, Iron Man appears in Capcom\'s <i>"Vs."</i> video games, including <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel_Super_Heroes_(arcade_game)" class="mw-redirect" title="Marvel Super Heroes (arcade game)">Marvel Super Heroes</a></i>,  <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel_vs._Capcom_2:_New_Age_of_Heroes" title="Marvel vs. Capcom 2: New Age of Heroes">Marvel vs. Capcom 2: New Age of Heroes</a></i>, <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel_vs._Capcom_3:_Fate_of_Two_Worlds" title="Marvel vs. Capcom 3: Fate of Two Worlds">Marvel vs. Capcom 3: Fate of Two Worlds</a></i>, and <i><a href="/wiki/Ultimate_Marvel_vs._Capcom_3" title="Ultimate Marvel vs. Capcom 3">Ultimate Marvel vs. Capcom 3</a></i>. Iron Man is a playable character in <i><a href="/wiki/Iron_Man_(video_game)" title="Iron Man (video game)">Iron Man</a></i>, the 1992 arcade game <i><a href="/wiki/Captain_America_and_the_Avengers" class="mw-redirect" title="Captain America and the Avengers">Captain America and the Avengers</a></i>, <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel:_Ultimate_Alliance" title="Marvel: Ultimate Alliance">Marvel: Ultimate Alliance</a></i> and <a href="/wiki/Marvel:_Ultimate_Alliance_2" title="Marvel: Ultimate Alliance 2">its sequel</a>, and <i><a href="/wiki/Marvel_Nemesis:_Rise_of_the_Imperfects" title="Marvel Nemesis: Rise of the Imperfects">Marvel Nemesis: Rise of the Imperfects</a></i>, as well as being featured as an unlockable character in <i><a href="/wiki/X-Men_Legends_II:_Rise_of_Apocalypse" title="X-Men Legends II: Rise of Apocalypse">X-Men Legends II: Rise of Apocalypse</a></i> and <i><a href="/wiki/Tony_Hawk%27s_Underground" title="Tony Hawk&#39;s Underground">Tony Hawk\'s Underground</a></i>.<sup id="cite_ref-142" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-142">&#91;142&#93;</a></sup> In the 2009 animated series, <i><a href="/wiki/Iron_Man:_Armored_Adventures" title="Iron Man: Armored Adventures">Iron Man: Armored Adventures</a></i>, most of the characters, including Tony Stark, are teenagers. An <a href="/wiki/Marvel_Anime" title="Marvel Anime">anime adaptation</a> began airing in Japan in October 2010 as part of a collaboration between <a href="/wiki/Marvel_Animation" title="Marvel Animation">Marvel Animation</a> and <a href="/wiki/Madhouse_(company)" title="Madhouse (company)">Madhouse</a>, in which Stark, voiced by <a href="/wiki/Keiji_Fujiwara" title="Keiji Fujiwara">Keiji Fujiwara</a>, travels to Japan where he ends up facing off against the Zodiac.<sup id="cite_ref-143" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-143">&#91;143&#93;</a></sup>\n</p>\n<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Iron_Man_Mk4_(14041231165).jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg/220px-Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="330" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg/330px-Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg/440px-Iron_Man_Mk4_%2814041231165%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2721" data-file-height="4081" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Iron_Man_Mk4_(14041231165).jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Iron Man suit Mark VI from Iron Man 2 (premiered in 2010)</div></div></div>\n<p>In 2008, a film adaptation titled <i><a href="/wiki/Iron_Man_(2008_film)" title="Iron Man (2008 film)">Iron Man</a></i> was released, starring <a href="/wiki/Robert_Downey_Jr." title="Robert Downey Jr.">Robert Downey Jr.</a> as Tony Stark and directed by <a href="/wiki/Jon_Favreau" title="Jon Favreau">Jon Favreau</a>. <i>Iron Man</i> received very positive reviews from film critics,<sup id="cite_ref-144" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-144">&#91;144&#93;</a></sup> grossing $318 million domestically and $585 million worldwide, and became the first in a series of over 20 films known as the <a href="/wiki/Marvel_Cinematic_Universe" title="Marvel Cinematic Universe">Marvel Cinematic Universe</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-boxmojo_145-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-boxmojo-145">&#91;145&#93;</a></sup> The character of Tony Stark, again played by Robert Downey Jr., appeared at the end of the 2008 film <i><a href="/wiki/The_Incredible_Hulk_(film)" title="The Incredible Hulk (film)">The Incredible Hulk</a></i>. Downey reprised his role in <i><a href="/wiki/Iron_Man_2" title="Iron Man 2">Iron Man 2</a></i> (2010), <i><a href="/wiki/The_Avengers_(2012_film)" title="The Avengers (2012 film)">Marvel\'s The Avengers</a></i> (2012), <i><a href="/wiki/Iron_Man_3" title="Iron Man 3">Iron Man 3</a></i> (2013), <i><a href="/wiki/Avengers:_Age_of_Ultron" title="Avengers: Age of Ultron">Avengers: Age of Ultron</a></i> (2015), <i><a href="/wiki/Captain_America:_Civil_War" title="Captain America: Civil War">Captain America: Civil War</a></i> (2016), <i><a href="/wiki/Spider-Man:_Homecoming" title="Spider-Man: Homecoming">Spider-Man: Homecoming</a></i> (2017), <i><a href="/wiki/Avengers:_Infinity_War" title="Avengers: Infinity War">Avengers: Infinity War</a></i> (2018), <i><a href="/wiki/Avengers:_Endgame" title="Avengers: Endgame">Avengers: Endgame</a></i> (2019), and the upcoming <i><a href="/wiki/Black_Widow_(2020_film)" title="Black Widow (2020 film)">Black Widow</a></i> (2020).<sup id="cite_ref-146" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-146">&#91;146&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-147" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-147">&#91;147&#93;</a></sup>\n</p><p>In 2009, Playtech released an online casino slot machine game called <i>Iron Man</i>. After that they created two more games, <i>Iron Man 2</i> and Iron Man 3.<sup id="cite_ref-148" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-148">&#91;148&#93;</a></sup>\n</p><p>In October 2016, <a href="/wiki/Eoin_Colfer" title="Eoin Colfer">Eoin Colfer</a> released a young adult novel called <i>Iron Man: The Gauntlet</i>.<sup id="cite_ref-149" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-149">&#91;149&#93;</a></sup>\n</p>\n<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Cultural_influence_and_legacy">Cultural influence and legacy</span></h2>\n<div class="quotebox pullquote floatright" style="width:33%;&#32;;"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r887775652">.mw-parser-output .quotebox{background-color:#F9F9F9;border:1px solid #aaa;box-sizing:border-box;padding:10px;font-size:88%;max-width:100%}.mw-parser-output .quotebox.floatleft{margin:0.5em 1.4em 0.8em 0}.mw-parser-output .quotebox.floatright{margin:0.5em 0 0.8em 1.4em}.mw-parser-output .quotebox.centered{margin:0.5em auto 0.8em auto}.mw-parser-output .quotebox.floatleft p,.mw-parser-output .quotebox.floatright p{font-style:inherit}.mw-parser-output .quotebox-title{background-color:#F9F9F9;text-align:center;font-size:larger;font-weight:bold}.mw-parser-output .quotebox-quote.quoted:before{font-family:"Times New Roman",serif;font-weight:bold;font-size:large;color:gray;content:" \xe2\x80\x9c ";vertical-align:-45%;line-height:0}.mw-parser-output .quotebox-quote.quoted:after{font-family:"Times New Roman",serif;font-weight:bold;font-size:large;color:gray;content:" \xe2\x80\x9d ";line-height:0}.mw-parser-output .quotebox .left-aligned{text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .quotebox .right-aligned{text-align:right}.mw-parser-output .quotebox .center-aligned{text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .quotebox cite{display:block;font-style:normal}@media screen and (max-width:360px){.mw-parser-output .quotebox{min-width:100%;margin:0 0 0.8em!important;float:none!important}}</style>\n<div class="quotebox-quote left-aligned" style="">Basically I\'m here to announce that we are building Iron Man.</div>\n<p><cite class="left-aligned" style="">\xe2\x80\x94 The <a href="/wiki/List_of_Presidents_of_the_United_States" class="mw-redirect" title="List of Presidents of the United States">44th</a> <a href="/wiki/President_of_the_United_States" title="President of the United States">President of the United States</a> <a href="/wiki/Barack_Obama" title="Barack Obama">Barack Obama</a> made a surprise announcement at a press conference at the <a href="/wiki/White_House" title="White House">White House</a>, when he revealed details of the creation of "Manufacturing Innovation Institutes" in <a href="/wiki/Chicago" title="Chicago">Chicago</a> and <a href="/wiki/Detroit" title="Detroit">Detroit</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-150" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-150">&#91;150&#93;</a></sup></cite>\n</p>\n</div>\n<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Rapping" title="Rapping">rapper</a> <a href="/wiki/Ghostface_Killah" title="Ghostface Killah">Ghostface Killah</a>, a member of <a href="/wiki/Wu-Tang_Clan" title="Wu-Tang Clan">Wu-Tang Clan</a>, titled his 1996 debut solo album <i><a href="/wiki/Ironman_(Ghostface_Killah_album)" title="Ironman (Ghostface Killah album)">Ironman</a></i>, and has since continued to use lyrics related to the Iron Man comics and <a href="/wiki/Sampling_(music)" title="Sampling (music)">samples</a> from the animated TV shows on his records.<sup id="cite_ref-151" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-151">&#91;151&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Shaheem_152-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Shaheem-152">&#91;152&#93;</a></sup> He has adopted the nickname Tony Starks as one of his numerous alter-egos,<sup id="cite_ref-Shaheem_152-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Shaheem-152">&#91;152&#93;</a></sup> and was featured in a scene deleted from the <a href="/wiki/Iron_Man_(2008_film)" title="Iron Man (2008 film)"><i>Iron Man</i> film</a>.</li>\n<li><a href="/wiki/Paul_McCartney" title="Paul McCartney">Paul McCartney</a>\'s song "<a href="/wiki/Magneto_and_Titanium_Man" title="Magneto and Titanium Man">Magneto and Titanium Man</a>" was inspired by the <a href="/wiki/X-Men" title="X-Men">X-Men</a>\'s nemesis and the original version of the Iron Man villain. Another Iron Man villain, the Crimson Dynamo, is mentioned in the lyrics to this song.<sup id="cite_ref-153" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-153">&#91;153&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-154" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-154">&#91;154&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>The British band <a href="/wiki/Razorlight" title="Razorlight">Razorlight</a> mentions Tony Stark in a verse of their song, "Hang By, Hang By".<sup id="cite_ref-155" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-155">&#91;155&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>The character of <a href="/wiki/Nathan_Stark" class="mw-redirect" title="Nathan Stark">Nathan Stark</a> on the television show <i><a href="/wiki/Eureka_(U.S._TV_series)" class="mw-redirect" title="Eureka (U.S. TV series)">Eureka</a></i> is inspired by Tony Stark.<sup id="cite_ref-156" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-156">&#91;156&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>In 2011, <a href="/wiki/IGN" title="IGN">IGN</a> ranked Iron Man 12th in the Top 100 Comic Book Heroes.<sup id="cite_ref-IGNTop100_2-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-IGNTop100-2">&#91;2&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>Two Iron Man-themed trucks compete in the <a href="/wiki/Monster_Jam" title="Monster Jam">Monster Jam</a> monster truck racing series. Debuted in Atlanta on 9 January 2010, they are driven by Lee O\' Donnell and Morgan Kane.<sup id="cite_ref-157" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-157">&#91;157&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>In 2015, <a href="/wiki/University_of_Central_Florida" title="University of Central Florida">University of Central Florida</a> engineering student Albert Manero, who builds and donates affordable <a href="/wiki/3D-printed" class="mw-redirect" title="3D-printed">3D-printed</a> bionic limbs to those in need, constructed a <a href="/wiki/Bionic_arm" class="mw-redirect" title="Bionic arm">bionic arm</a> based on Iron Man\'s suit for 7-year-old Alex Pring, a superhero fan who was born with a partially formed right arm. He then delivered the Iron Man arm to Pring with the help of Robert Downey Jr. in character as Tony Stark.<sup id="cite_ref-158" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-158">&#91;158&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>For <a href="/wiki/Major_League_Baseball" title="Major League Baseball">Major League Baseball</a> <a href="/wiki/Players_Weekend" title="Players Weekend">Players Weekend</a> in August 2018, <a href="/wiki/New_York_Yankees" title="New York Yankees">New York Yankees</a> second baseman <a href="/wiki/Gleyber_Torres" title="Gleyber Torres">Gleyber Torres</a> designed his custom cleats after Iron Man\'s suit.<sup id="cite_ref-159" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-159">&#91;159&#93;</a></sup></li>\n<li>A TV show demonstrated a <a href="/wiki/Hover_(helicopter)" class="mw-redirect" title="Hover (helicopter)">hovering</a> <a href="/wiki/Bulletproofing" title="Bulletproofing">bullet-mitigating</a> suit with some of the official Marvel design features in 2019.<sup id="cite_ref-160" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-160">&#91;160&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-161" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-161">&#91;161&#93;</a></sup></li></ul>\n<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="See_also">See also</span></h2>\n<ul><li><a href="/wiki/List_of_Iron_Man_enemies" title="List of Iron Man enemies">List of Iron Man enemies</a></li></ul>\n<div style="clear:both;"></div>\n<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="References">References</span></h2>\n<div class="reflist columns references-column-width" style="-moz-column-width: 30em;
